\goodbreak\line{\hskip-4.6cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\hreg\hfill COMMUNIA\hfill}\hss}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {COM0} fit
\vskip-.6cm
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
In memoriis, antiphonæ ad\/ {\reg Benedictus} et\/ {\reg Magnificat,} si non sunt propriæ, sumuntur aut 
de Communi aut de feria currente. Si de Communi, antiphonæ ad\/ {\reg Magnificat,} quæ pro I 
Vesperis indicantur, possunt adhiberi ad libitum ad Vesperas. Licet insuper seligere utramque 
antiphonam ex iis quæ ad calcem uniuscuiusque Communis ad libitum proponuntur.
}\vskip.4cm%
\goodbreak\line{\hskip-4.6cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\mmed\hfill Dedicationis ecclesiæ\hfill}\hss}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {COM1} fit
